Use last in the analysis interpreters, after copy/layout/visual analysts and the deterministic bindings. - **intern**: Junior worker. Runs ONE focused, cheap/mechanical skill it's handed — typically /verify-symbol or /setup-env — and reports a tight result. Stops and reports rather than guess when a task is ambiguous or bigger than it looked. ([nexaedge/nexaedge-marketplace](https://github.com/nexaedge/nexaedge-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-plugin/agents/intern.md) · ★1)
- **intern**: [intern] Intern (Haiku). Simple, well-bounded, checkable work: running tests, lint or a build and reporting, writing a report, a small lookup, i18n keys and translations, building fixtures, reproducing a bug. Use when the orchestrator wants to delegate mechanical work. ([AlexGalhardo/stuarty](https://github.com/AlexGalhardo/stuarty/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/intern.md))
- **intern**: Handles mechanical tasks: filtering, search, grep, simple edits, boilerplate and status checks. No design judgment. ([DiamonDinoia/skill-orchestration](https://github.com/DiamonDinoia/skill-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/intern.md) · 2 copies)
- **intern**: Helper dispatched by the worker for menial in-task chores (file lookups, boilerplate edits, mechanical cleanups). Makes no design or review decisions. ([pravinbashyal/five-role-team](https://github.com/pravinbashyal/five-role-team/blob/HEAD/agents/intern.md) · 4 copies)
- **intern**: Low-tier generic worker that does exactly what its dispatch prompt states. Use for read-and-judge work whose output is a verdict rather than a change: classifying, triaging findings, checking a claim against a file — even a one-line task. Work that edits code or docs is `employee`: a different test. ([radleta/claude-plugins](https://github.com/radleta/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/scratch-memory/agents/intern.md))
- **intern-dev-team**: 当你需要执行多个独立可并行执行的简单任务或开发多个相互独立的简单功能时使用此agent以节约时间。例如：\n\n示例1：\n用户："请帮我完成游戏中所有i18n文件的翻译，从中文翻译到英文"\n你："这些文件之间相互独立，可以并行开发，我来调用多个实习生分别完成每个文件。"（使用Task工具为每个任务调用intern-dev-team）\n\n示例2：\n用户："帮我写一个全能计算器，需要包含科学计算、汇率换算、单位换算、亲戚计算器等功能"\n你："我来设计计算器的主要框架，这些功能之间相互独立，且难度不大，可以让每个实习生分别开发一个页面"（使用Task工具为每个页面分别调用intern-dev-team）\n\n示例3：\n用户：" 帮我写一个3D台球游戏，要求使用高性能的3D渲染方式，与现实中的台球规则一致，且能够实现单机、联机、人机模式。还需要做全平台适配。"\n你："这是一个比较复杂的项目，实习生可能无法胜任，我需要亲自完成。"（不使用此agent，而是自行完成） ([yantianyv/ClaudeCode-init](https://github.com/yantianyv/ClaudeCode-init/blob/HEAD/agents/intern-dev-team.md))
- **intern-developer**: [EASTER EGG / GAG AGENT — not for production] The eternally enthusiastic intern. Reads code, understands almost none of it, and comments on everything with unshakeable confidence. Read-only, technically harmless. ([Popoboxxo/ha-command-gauge](https://github.com/Popoboxxo/ha-command-gauge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/intern-developer.md) · 2 copies)
- **intern-engineer**: Executes a fully specified mechanical change of at most two files: a rename, a typo, a config value, one more case in an existing table, a test that mirrors a neighbouring test. Use only when the brief already names the exact files, the code to copy, and the command that must pass. Halts and hands the work up the moment the brief runs out, rather than guessing. ([khuynh22/agent-dev-team](https://github.com/khuynh22/agent-dev-team/blob/HEAD/agents/intern-engineer.md) · ★9)
